# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 1,
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeColor

Example:

```

## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, or one emoji/icon grapheme, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"AB"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountEnvKey.json), input validation.

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "authMethod": "unknown",
  "availability": "available",
  "id": "example",
  "installationId": "example",
  "isDefault": false,
  "label": "example",
  "profileRevision": "example",
  "provider": "acp",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "unknown",
    "blockers": {},
    "observedAt": 4,
    "plan": "example",
    "usage": {
      "inputTokens": 7
    },
    "windows": {}
  }
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": true,
    "resume": false
  },
  "coverage": "generic",
  "mcp": "http",
  "modeSelection": true,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": true
  },
  "subagentSessions": false,
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |

| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

```json

  "native": {
    "aliases": [],

    "nativeId": "example",
    "provider": "cursor"
  },

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

}

| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| all | no | `true` |  |

| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| recovery | yes | ["pause","headless"] |  |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| pageStateLost | yes | `true` |  |
| reason | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |

  "type": "browser.backend.lost",
  "url": "example"
}
```

## BrowserOrigin

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOrigin.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An exact canonical HTTP(S) origin, including scheme and optional port, with no path, query, fragment or URL credentials.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"https://youtube.com"
```

## BrowserState

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| owner | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| closed | yes | boolean |  |
| backend | no | ["embedded","headless"] |  |
| status | no | ["ready","paused","recovering"] |  |
| reason | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| pageStateLost | no | boolean |  |
| loading | no | boolean |  |
| loadError | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| permissionDenied | no | object | {"properties":{"permission":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"origin":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["permission","origin"]} |
| blocked | no | [BrowserOriginBlock.json](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json) |  |
| activeTabId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tabs | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserTab.json"}} |
| downloads | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserDownload.json"}} |
| pending_dialog | no | [BrowserDialog.json](schema/BrowserDialog.json) |  |
| takeoverMode | no | ["shared","private"] |  |

Example:

}

| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

  }
}
```

Semantic rule: seq must equal view.seq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"snapshot"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |

  "subscriptionId": "example",

    "kind": "threads",
    "seq": 7,

      "before": {
        "at": 9,
        "id": "example"
      },
      "counts": [],
      "total": 7
    }
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "afterSeq": 4,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 7,
  "type": "events"
}
```

Semantic rule: throughSeq must be >= afterSeq.

  "type": "progress"
}

## DiagnosticsHealth

[JSON Schema](schema/DiagnosticsHealth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| at | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| eventLoop | yes | object | {"properties":{"meanMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"p99Ms":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"maxMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["meanMs","p99Ms","maxMs"]} |
| memory | yes | object | {"properties":{"rssBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapUsedBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapTotalBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["rssBytes","heapUsedBytes","heapTotalBytes"]} |
| openHandles | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sqlite | yes | object | {"properties":{"pageBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"walBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["pageBytes","walBytes"]} |
| activeSessions | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| queues | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"maxProperties":64,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 64 queue entries."} |
| logs | yes | object | {"properties":{"dropped":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"failed":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"queued":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"directory":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["dropped","failed","queued"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "activeSessions": 6,
  "at": 4,
  "eventLoop": {
    "maxMs": 2,
    "meanMs": 8,
    "p99Ms": 4
  },
  "logs": {
    "directory": "example",
    "dropped": 5,
    "failed": 3,
    "queued": 9
  },
  "memory": {
    "heapTotalBytes": 7,
    "heapUsedBytes": 0,
    "rssBytes": 6
  },
  "openHandles": 1,
  "queues": {},
  "sqlite": {
    "pageBytes": null,
    "walBytes": 5
  }
}
```

| id | yes | [InteractionId.json](schema/InteractionId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| toolCallId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| blocking | yes | boolean |  |
| request | yes | [InteractionRequest.json](schema/InteractionRequest.json) |  |
| review | no | [PermissionReview.json](schema/PermissionReview.json) |  |
| state | yes | [InteractionState.json](schema/InteractionState.json) |  |
| expirationReason | no | `"provider_disconnected"` |  |
| resolution | no | [InteractionResolution.json](schema/InteractionResolution.json) |  |
| resolvedBy | no | [DeviceId.json](schema/DeviceId.json) |  |
| autoReviewed | no | boolean |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| closedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "blocking": true,
  "createdAt": 0,
  "id": "example",
  "raw": [],
  "request": {
    "description": "example",
    "kind": "elicitation",
    "message": "example",
    "server": "example",
    "url": "example"
  },
  "state": "expired",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## InteractionEvidence

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionEvidence.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| updatesMs | yes | array | {"maxItems":2400,"items":{"type":"number","minimum":0,"maximum":10000}} |
| actionAtMs | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| cores | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| truncated | no | boolean |  |
| refreshAssumed | no | boolean |  |
| notes | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":24,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "actionAtMs": 8,
  "cores": 3,
  "hostLoad": 3,
  "layoutShift": 4,
  "longTasks": [],
  "refreshHz": 1,
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "window",
    "windowId": 4
  },
  "truncated": false,
  "updatesMs": [],
  "windowMs": 5
}
```

## InteractionMeasurement

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionMeasurement.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| source | yes | ["screen-frames","browser-trace"] |  |
| target | yes | [InteractionTarget.json](schema/InteractionTarget.json) |  |
| action | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenInput.json"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserInteractionAction.json"}]} |
| refreshHz | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":240} |
| windowMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":10000} |
| latencyMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| settleMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| frames | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":2400} |
| effectiveFps | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| droppedFrames | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hitches | yes | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| hitchRatioMsPerS | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| hitchTimeMs | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| longTasks | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionGap.json"}} |
| layoutShift | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| verdict | yes | [InteractionVerdict.json](schema/InteractionVerdict.json) |  |
| confidence | yes | [InteractionConfidence.json](schema/InteractionConfidence.json) |  |
| hostLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| captureOverheadPct | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| filmstrip | no | [InteractionFilmstrip.json](schema/InteractionFilmstrip.json) |  |
| notes | yes | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}} |
| repeat | no | [InteractionRepeat.json](schema/InteractionRepeat.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "captureOverheadPct": 1,
  "confidence": "high",
  "droppedFrames": 2,
  "effectiveFps": 5,
  "frames": 7,
  "hitches": [],
  "longTasks": [],
  "notes": [],
  "refreshHz": 1,
  "settleMs": 7,
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "window",
    "windowId": 5
  },
  "verdict": "inconclusive",
  "windowMs": 3
}
```

## InteractionRepeat

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "metrics": {
    "captureOverheadPct": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 4
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 1
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 9
    },
    "hitchRatioMsPerS": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 4
    },
    "longTaskMs": {
      "median": 8,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 4
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "high",
      "verdict": "inconclusive"
    }
  ]
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |

| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 2,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 4,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 5,
  "version": 1
}
```

## ProjectIcon

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Semantic rule: Absolute HTTP or HTTPS WHATWG URL, without ASCII tabs, CR, LF or trailing whitespace; at most 4096 UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce URL parsing in application code.

Example:

```json
"https://example.invalid/icon.png"
```

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

## PromptArgument

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptArgument.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An argument default must have the declared type.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | ["string","number","boolean"] |  |
| required | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| default | no | [PromptValue.json](schema/PromptValue.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "default": false,
  "required": false,
  "type": "boolean"
}
```

## ProviderConfiguration

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |

Example:

  "instance": "{y|yyzy",
  "provider": "cursor",
  "shownGroups": []
}

| prerequisite | no | object | {"properties":{"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"sourceUrl":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}},"required":["name","sourceUrl"]} |

| verify | no | [InstallCommand.json](schema/InstallCommand.json) |  |

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderKind.json), input validation.

Type: ["claude","codex","opencode","cursor","antigravity","acp","pi"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"claude"

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderLoginProgress.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| session | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |

| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |
| userCode | no | string | {"pattern":"^[A-Z0-9]{4,5}-[A-Z0-9]{4,5}$"} |
| prompt | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |

[JSON Schema](schema/RemoteTask.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| rootThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| parentPermissionMode | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},{"type":"null"}]} |
| parentPermissionOverride | no | union | {"default":null,"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},{"type":"null"}]} |
| parentThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentAgentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| request | yes | [RemoteDelegationRequest.json](schema/RemoteDelegationRequest.json) |  |
| permissionMode | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| usage | no | [RemoteTaskUsage.json](schema/RemoteTaskUsage.json) | {"default":{"tokens":0,"cost":0}} |
| phase | yes | [RemoteTaskPhase.json](schema/RemoteTaskPhase.json) |  |
| dispatched | yes | boolean |  |
| result | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| truncated | no | boolean |  |
| artifactsUnavailable | no | boolean |  |
| delivered | yes | boolean |  |
| createdAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| cancellingAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| finishedAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

    "attachments": [],

    "parentThreadId": "example",

  "parentPermissionMode": "example",

  "parentThreadId": "example",
  "permissionMode": "example",
  "phase": "failed",
  "request": {

    "hostId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "mode": "worktree",
    "provider": "cursor",
    "requestId": "dVyG",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "result": "example",
  "rootThreadId": "example",
  "sourceHostId": "example",
  "threadId": "example"
}

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |
| fingerprint | yes | [ReviewFingerprint.json](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ]
}
```

| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

Example:

```json

  "end": 1,
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |

| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |

| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| captureGeneration | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| ts | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| width | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":3840} |
| height | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":2160} |
| scale | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8} |
| codec | yes | ["jpeg","h264"] |  |
| keyframe | no | boolean |  |
| videoCodec | no | string | {"pattern":"^avc1\\.[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"} |
| dirtyRects | no | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenRect.json"}} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8388608} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 4,
  "codec": "jpeg",
  "height": 7,
  "scale": 1,
  "seq": 6,
  "sessionId": "wOQQqQ5Dg_7mXj6HBsmeLxA4KRlRecnpSsUrFBtgVA",
  "ts": 7,
  "version": 2,
  "width": 2
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":9,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "before": 1,
  "kind": "reasoning",
  "kinds": [
    "tool_call"
  ],
  "provider": "antigravity",
  "threadId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

  "threads.settleOnClose": false,

}

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. | steps | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"step":{"type":"string","enum":["preparing","fetching","creating","checking_out","setup","done"]},"startedAt":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"elapsedMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["step","startedAt","elapsedMs"]}} |
| details | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}} |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| cleanupComplete | yes | boolean |  |
| actions | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["cancel","local","retry"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "actions": [],
  "attempt": 0,
  "cleanupComplete": false,
  "commandId": "example",
  "details": [],
  "elapsedMs": 7,
  "startedAt": 0,
  "state": "running",
  "step": "creating",
  "steps": []
}
```
